1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a time_series algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 811. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 527/1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 811 + O(1/n^527). Summing over all modes and applying the time_series convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
